- **2026-05-17 — `/notes` — NDA Maths Statistics teaching notes pilot.** Public per-subtopic teaching content, dual-purpose for digital-board lectures (Present mode) and student self-study (Read mode). 4 subtopics × 21 concept units × 21 authored worked examples × **89 curated PYQ UUIDs** (8 pyqExamples + 81 per-concept drill UUIDs). The atomic block is `ConceptUnit`, NOT the subtopic — each unit renders as a self-contained mini-lesson (intuition → definition → boxed formula with symbol legend → step-by-step authored example with always-visible answer → bank PYQ via WorkedExampleCard → traps → inline "Drill N more →" link). Present mode is a client island (`NotePresenter`) that derives a slide deck from the same data via the pure `splitNoteIntoSlides` helper (18 TDD tests; ordering: title → why → for each concept (intro → authored → pyq → traps → concept-drill) → drill). Keyboard nav ←/→/Space/Shift+Space/F/Esc. **Performance:** every /notes page exports `revalidate = 3600` and uses the new cookie-free `createSupabaseAnonClient` (cookie-aware client forced dynamic rendering even though the page is anon-only). Module-level `getNotesTaxonomy` memoises the 3-query taxonomy resolve. Prod warm ~200ms; cold first-hit ~1.2s. **Cross-links:** notes pill on /browse Hero alongside the guide pill, "Statistics Notes" link in Footer, banner on /guide/nda-maths landing. `/notes` and `/notes/nda-maths` 307-redirect to `/notes/nda-maths/statistics` (only chapter live). Sitemap + robots updated. **Integrity check:** `scripts/notes-lint.ts` (`npm run notes:lint`) validates every `subtopicName` resolves under live taxonomy AND every UUID (`pyqExampleId` + `drillQuestionIds`) is PUBLIC. Catches the "subtopic rename → silent breakage" failure mode from the recurring-pitfalls section. **Hybrid drill option chosen for pilot** (vs full DB concept-tags table): per-concept `drillQuestionIds: string[]` curated by hand in TS. Forward-compatible with a future `question_concept_tags` table — migration would be one INSERT per pair. For the 4-subtopic pilot, ~140 manual picks were cheap; the DB table only pays off past ~20 notes. **Why NOT unified with /guide principles** (initial unified-table proposal, reversed mid-conversation): principles are **horizontal** (cross-subtopic, ~20 hand-curated, value = selectivity); concepts are **vertical** (within-subtopic, thousands at scale, value = coverage). Different axes → never share a schema; can share admin UI later. See [[principle-vs-concept-tags]] memory. **Reused unchanged from /guide infra:** GuideShell, BrowseLink, loadWorkedExamples, resolveTaxonomy. **New:** ConceptUnitCard, NotePresenter, FormulaBlock (with symbol legend), TrapCallout, WorkedExampleAuthored. `WorkedExampleCard` gained an optional `presentMode` prop that scales fonts for digital-board projection.
- **2026-05-17 — Concept-tags infra Phase 1 (migration 0021).** Reverses the pilot decision to keep drill UUIDs in TS — DB-backed concept tagging is the load-bearing infra to scale /notes past hand-curated lists. New table `question_concept_tags(question_id, subtopic_slug, concept_slug, tagged_by, tagged_at, tagged_by_llm)` with composite PK + reverse-lookup index. **RLS:** anon reads tags on PUBLIC questions, authed reads also include own-org PRIVATE, ADMIN writes scoped to own-org questions (7-test RLS smoke covers all combos). **Helper module** `src/lib/tags/conceptTags.ts`: `getTagsForQuestion`, `setTagsForQuestion` (diffs added/removed/kept; idempotent), `getQuestionIdsForConcept`, `loadConceptDrills` (the Phase 2 reader; 9-test integration suite). **Edit flow extension:** `PUT /api/questions/[id]` accepts optional `conceptTags: { subtopicSlug, conceptSlug }[]`; `applyEdit` validates each pair against `subtopicSlugRegistry.validateConceptTag` (subtopic-slug must match the question's final subtopic; concept-slug must exist in the chapter notes module); new `invalid_concept_tag` discriminated-union variant returns 400. Omitted `conceptTags` field = leave tags alone; `[]` = clear; subtopic change in same save = silently skip tag write (with a UI hint to re-edit). **Admin UI:** new "Concepts" section in `EditQuestionForm`. Renders concept-list checkboxes when the question's subtopic has notes content, otherwise shows a hint. **Backfill:** `scripts/migrate-notes-drills-to-tags.ts` + `npm run tags:migrate`. Initial run wrote 90 rows from existing Statistics curation (central-tendency 45, dispersion 27, regression-correlation 11, frequency-distributions 7). Idempotent — re-run = 0 new. **What's intentionally NOT in Phase 1:** /notes pages still read `drillQuestionIds` from TS modules (Phase 2 will swap `ConceptUnitCard` and slide deck to `loadConceptDrills`); notes-lint still validates TS-side UUIDs (will pivot to DB-tag integrity in Phase 2). **LLM-suggest pipeline** (originally proposed as Phase 3 — automated upload-time tagging UI): killed as a feature; replaced with chat-driven tagging sessions per chapter using the same inline-iteration pattern as taxonomy cleanup. Zero new code; admins also have the per-question UI for ad-hoc tagging. **Test coverage** added: 7 RLS + 9 helper + 4 applyEdit-tags = 20 new tests; full 42-test edit suite green.
- **2026-05-17 — Concept-tags infra Phase 2 (DB-backed drills on /notes).** Closes the loop on Phase 1 — /notes pages now read concept drill lists from `question_concept_tags` at request time, not from TS modules. **New helper** `src/lib/notes/loadResolvedDrills.ts` (5-test integration suite): calls `loadConceptDrills`, filters each concept's `pyqExampleId` out of its drill list (the featured PYQ is shown above the drill in read mode — no duplicate), sorts remaining UUIDs ascending for stability, returns `Map<conceptSlug, string[]>`. Concepts with zero remaining tagged questions are omitted (silent absence — UI hides the "Drill N more →" link). **Signature change:** `splitNoteIntoSlides(note, drillsByConcept)` now takes the resolved map as a second arg; `ConceptUnitCard` takes a `drillQuestionIds: string[]` prop. Slide-test suite rebuilt: 19 tests passing. **Hard-removed `drillQuestionIds`** from the `ConceptUnit` type — typecheck enforced the cleanup across 21 occurrences in 4 data files (central-tendency 8, dispersion 6, regression-correlation 4, frequency-distributions 3). **One-shot backfill script deleted** (`scripts/migrate-notes-drills-to-tags.ts` + `npm run tags:migrate`) — served its purpose in Phase 1, the 90 rows it wrote are now the authoritative source. **Notes-lint pivoted** to Phase-2-aware integrity: validates every DB tag's `concept_slug` matches a known TS concept (error — catches orphan tags after rename); soft-warns when a TS concept has 0 DB-tagged questions (catches missed tagging sessions). pyqExampleId-PUBLIC check kept; old drillQuestionIds-UUID-PUBLIC check removed. **Net effect:** authoring a new chapter is now write-the-TS-module + run-a-tagging-session-in-chat; new tags propagate to live pages within the 1-hour ISR window. **50/50 tests green** (notes + tags + edit suite); prepush clean; `npm run notes:lint` against 4 notes + 21 concepts + 90 DB tags = 0 errors / 0 warnings.

SET DEFAULT 'PRIVATE'` restores the old default; per-row flips remain available via the edit page. **Premium/org-only content path** (if ever needed): explicit `visibility = 'PRIVATE'` on insert + per-row UI flip. Cascaded the corrected numbers through 6 files: `STRATEGY_HEADLINE` constants (paperQ / totalMarks / targetMarks / targetAttempts updated to Chem 15-60-45-13 and Bio 11-44-32-10); strategy-page arithmetic table (the 75 / 90 / 95% accuracy rows had Physics-shaped Net-marks values — recomputed against the new per-paper totals); "attempt 20, skip 5" prose → "attempt 13 of 15, skip 2" (Chem) / "attempt 10 of 11, skip 1" (Bio); "~25-min slot" narrative → ~15 min (Chem) / ~10 min (Bio) with new explicit calibration that the 150-min GAT is split by section q-count; "Target: 70+ marks" footer → "Target: 45+ marks (of 60 max)" / "30+ marks (of 44 max)"; TEST_DAY_PLAN slot durations + per-phase q-count narratives recalibrated (Chem 5+7+3=15 min · Bio 4+5+2=11 min); TIME_BUDGET outcomes per strand (~14 marks/paper etc) recomputed at 85% accuracy across the strand's per-paper q-share — sums now approximately match the target rather than under-promising at 25% of paper; per-chapter `expectedYieldPerPaper` recalibrated to 85% accuracy on each chapter's per-paper q count (Chem Carbon "4-5" → "~8" since 2.5 q/paper × 0.85 × 4 ≈ 8.5; Bio Human Physiology "5-6" → "~10" since 2.9 × 0.85 × 4 ≈ 9.9); trends.ts module-comments. Added explicit disambiguation in both module-comments: NDA PART B has multiple science sections (Physics ~25 q, Chemistry ~15 q, Biology ~10–11 q), each contributing independently to the 400-mark Part B / 600-mark GAT total — don't conflate. **The recurring failure mode**: copy-paste between sibling-guide strategy modules is fast, but the headline NUMBERS depend on the subject's per-paper q-count, which differs by section. Verify with `SELECT subject, AVG(q_per_paper), MIN(q_per_paper), MAX(q_per_paper) FROM (... GROUP BY subject, pyq_year, pyq_month, pyq_note) GROUP BY subject` before shipping any new guide's STRATEGY_HEADLINE. See [[per-paper-q-counts]] memory.
- **2026-05-18 — `/guide/nda-geography` 6-section strategy guide shipped end-to-end + Template B + non-flat %HARD variant registered + `/guide/nda` bumped 5→6 cards (commit c51ccd8).** Sixth guide subject under NDA — first PART A subject; first Template B guide with non-flat %HARD. **15 indexable pages**: geography landing + 5 sections (strategy, playbooks index, **reference-tables**, trends, traps) + **7 chapter playbook detail pages** at /playbooks/[slug] pre-rendered via generateStaticParams. **Template B variant 3** chosen after analysis showed Geography falls between pure B and C: bank has sealed chapters (max cross-chapter lever rivers-water 27 q × 4 ch — fails A's 40 q × ≥4 ch gate) BUT %HARD is non-flat (4 of 7 chapters >15% HARD — Climatology 28%, IG Economy 24%, Earth's Structure 20%, Earth in Space 18%; pure-B requires ≤2). Template C also rejected because HARD is **diffuse within chapters** rather than concentrated in 1–2 subtopics per chapter — Climatology HARD spread 4-3-3-3-2-1 across 6 subs, Earth's Structure 5-4-3-2-1-0-0, no clean cherry-pick pattern that DrillPosture overlay could exploit. **The variant**: Template B routes + per-chapter playbooks + multi-domain `/reference-tables` page, BUT strand-level %HARD calibration (Apply strand carries 23% avg HARD = densest strand, called out explicitly in strategy hero subtitle) in place of per-chapter DrillPosture. **Strand split = Recall (173 q · 50%, IG Economy + IG Physical + World/Human) / Apply (131 q · 38%, Climatology + Earth's Structure — densest HARD) / Verify (41 q · 12%, Earth in Space + Oceanography — multi-statement evaluation dominant)**. Same Recall/Apply/Verify shape as Biology — the initial "Geography might be Map/Concept/Current-Events" guess from the old `feedback_english_guide_structure_diverges` memory was wrong; SQL execution-mode probe showed multi-statement at 71 q dominates non-recall, far larger than location-anchor at 35 q, so Verify fits better than Locate as the third slot. **PART A not PART B**: per-paper avg 19.2 q (range 17–21) × 4 marks = ~76 max marks/paper (largest GAT section after English). STRATEGY_HEADLINE set to paperQ 19, totalMarks 76, targetMarks 50, targetAttempts 16, accuracy 85% — derived per [[per-paper-q-counts]] recipe (SQL-verified, not copy-pasted). **Largest chapter = Indian Geography Economy at 81 q · 24% HARD** — densest-HARD recall chapter in any guide so far, drives the warning in the Recall strand pitch. **Trends headline = "paper has NOT consistently hardened"** (same framing as Chemistry/Biology, opposite of Physics) but with extra qualifier — %HARD bounces 6%→42% with NO monotonic trajectory; 2018 outlier high (16 HARDs in 38 q), 2025 also high (12 HARDs in 40 q · 30%), 2019 + 2021 unusually easy. Don't over-weight recent. **Reference-tables = 62 named-fact pairs in 4 themed clusters** (Indian Rivers & Tributaries 15 / Indian Mountain Peaks & Ranges 13 / Mineral & Crop Producer States 17 / Local Winds + Climate Zones 17). Rendered via new `GeographyReferenceTables` component — sister to `BiologyReferenceTables`, same multi-domain shape (per-cluster column headers because Geography facts span 4 distinct domains too). Kept as parallel component (not parameterised BiologyReferenceTables) per "link path hard-coded per guide" precedent — now 6 RelatedX parallels + 2 ReferenceTables parallels. **Trap-page MIXED genre** (not pure paired-fact like Biology nor pure formula-misapplication like Physics): 11 shapes — Recall traps are paired-fact-swap (mineral↔state, river↔state, peak↔range, crop↔season, world river/canal, scheme over-inclusion); Apply traps are mechanism-direction flips (Coriolis flip, warm/cold current swap, plate-boundary outcome, S-wave-through-liquid, rock-classification error, temperature-inversion misread); Verify traps are multi-statement (partial-credit, terrestrial↔Jovian planet trait swap, universal-claim, spring-tide season confusion, Hawaii-on-ridge). 14 worked-example UUIDs SQL-picked against the live 345-q bank. **Plumbing**: sitemap +/guide/nda-geography + 5 sections + 7 detail pages (GEOGRAPHY_PLAYBOOK_SLUGS); Footer adds NDA Geography Guide link (Globe icon); /browse Hero adds nda-geography pill; /guide/nda-maths landing adds 5th cross-link banner (Globe); /guide/nda landing bumped 5→6 cards (xl:grid-cols-5 → xl:grid-cols-3 = 2×3 layout). **TDD-first**: wrote `tests/guide-nda-geography-playbooks.test.ts` (mirror of english + physics + chemistry + biology tests) BEFORE the data modules — 5 invariants including live-taxonomy resolution. All 587/587 tests pass; prepush clean (139 static pages). **The Template-B-with-non-flat-%HARD lesson**: when %HARD non-flat BUT HARD is diffuse within chapters, the Template C DrillPosture overlay has weak leverage. Strand-level calibration (densest-HARD strand called out explicitly) is the lighter-weight signal that works.
- **2026-05-18 — Tier 1 UI overhaul Phase 1: primary nav + cookie-backed exam pill (commit 69f23b0).** AppHeader gains 3 inline tabs (Bank · Guides · Notes) + an always-visible `ExamPill` Radix Popover. Pure helpers `src/lib/exam/examContext.ts`: `EXAM_REGISTRY` (slug + display name + per-exam route map), `resolveBankHref` / `resolveGuidesHref` / `resolveNotesHref` (fall back to `/guide` and `/notes` indexes for exams without subtrees), `getActiveTab` (path-segment match, defends against `/browser-other` false positives). Server orchestrator `loadActiveExamContext` reads cookie → validates → fetches exam UUID via cookie-free anon client → pre-builds the 3 hrefs. Pill writes `qb_exam` cookie (underscore for RFC-safety; not the colon used in the localStorage `qb:cart:v1` precedent since cookie names are RFC 6265 tokens) + calls `router.refresh()`. PrimaryNav is a client island; AppHeader stays Server Component. 24 unit tests for the pure helpers. Wordmark hides < sm so brand + 3 tabs + pill + avatar fit at 360px. No DB queries beyond the existing AppHeader-level work.
- **2026-05-18 — Tier 1 Phase 3: per-question backlink chips on QuestionCard (commit 69f23b0).** Server-side `src/lib/links/questionResources.ts` maps `(examName, subjectName, chapterName, subtopicName) → {guide, notes}`. Chapter→playbook lookup built once at module load from each guide's `PLAYBOOKS` array; subtopic→notes via `subtopicSlugRegistry` + the new shared `notesIndex.ts`. NDA Maths is Template A so the chip points at the overview, not a per-chapter playbook. QuestionList computes the map server-side and passes `resources?: QuestionResources` to QuestionCard; cards render a thin dashed-top chip row between header and expand panel only when at least one link exists. Editorial data stays out of the client bundle. 21 mapping tests covering all 6 NDA guide subjects + MHT-CET fallback + null subtopic + History/Polity/Economics/CA (no guides yet).
- **2026-05-18 — Tier 1 Phase 2: `/nda` exam home (commit 69f23b0).** New public exam-landing page at `/nda` — Hero + live total-q stat via new `getExamHomeStats(client, examName)` helper (head count, not `.select(...)` — same PostgREST 1000-row trap defence as `get_dashboard_stats`) + dual CTA (Build a paper · All six guides) + Strategy-guides grid (6 compact cards) + Teaching-notes grid (Statistics + Vectors) + closing back-to-bank banner. ISR `revalidate = 3600`, cookie-free anon client. Distinct purple OG image gradient. `/browse` Hero gains a Compass-icon "NDA preparation home" pill at the top of the pill row (slightly bolder than the guide pills). Sitemap +/nda at priority 0.95. Deferred: per-exam `/browse` route, brand-link → exam home, cookie-driven resume.
- **2026-05-18 — Tier 1 Phase 4: two-way notes ↔ guide cross-links (commit 69f23b0).** Extracted the "which chapters have shipped notes" lookup from `questionResources.ts` into a shared `src/lib/links/notesIndex.ts` exposing `hasChapterNotes`, `getNotesChapterHref`, `getNotesChapterLabel`, `getNotesChapterEntry`. Both `/notes` chapter index pages (Statistics + Vectors) and both subtopic pages gain a "NDA Maths strategy" + "NDA home" backlink chip row right under the GuideHero. `/guide/nda-maths` CHAPTER_TABLE rows for Statistics + Vectors render a small primary-bordered "📝 Notes" chip next to the chapter name. 6 tests for the shared lookup. The three surfaces (Bank · Guides · Notes) now feel like one product instead of three trees.
- **2026-05-18 — Tier 1.5: tag-depth backlinks on QuestionCard (commit e7ffe97).** `getQuestionResources` signature extended to accept optional `tags: ResourceTags`. When present and a TOP_11 principle slug resolves via `getPrincipleName`, the generic "NDA Maths strategy" chip is replaced with "Lever: <principle name>" → `/guide/nda-maths/principles/<slug>`. When a concept tag resolves via `getConceptName`, the generic "Concept notes" chip is replaced with "Concept: <concept name>" → `/notes/.../<subtopicSlug>#<conceptSlug>`. First tag wins; un-resolved slugs fall through to the generic chip. New batched `getResourceTagsForQuestions(client, ids)` fires from `/browse` after the questions query (2 parallel SELECTs against the indexed tag tables, returns `Map<questionId, ResourceTags>`). Long-tail principles (not in TOP_11) and unknown subjects keep the generic chip — graceful fallback throughout. 22 new tests (8 helper + 7 batch + 7 tag-name lookups). One extra DB round-trip per /browse render.
- **2026-05-18 — Refactor: `NOTES_CHAPTERS` single-source-of-truth registry (commit c179fbd).** Adding a `/notes` chapter previously required edits across 6 files (subtopicSlugRegistry, notesIndex, tagNames, sitemap, the chapter index page, scripts/notes-lint) — each independently re-imported the chapter's `_data` block, and forgetting one meant silent breakage. New `src/lib/notes/chapters.ts` carries `NOTES_CHAPTERS: readonly NotesChapterRegistration[]` (each entry: examName + subjectName + subjectRoute + chapterSlug + chipLabel + chapter + notes + slugs) plus `getNotesChapterBySlug` and `getNotesChaptersForSubject`. All 6 consumers refactored to derive. API-preserving — every existing test in the consumers passed unchanged. Adding a new chapter is now: write `_data/` + page templates, append ONE entry to `NOTES_CHAPTERS`, run tagging session. 9 new registry tests. The `/nda` `NOTES_PREVIEWS` array still has editorial blurbs hand-written; deferred for now since the blurbs are display content, not structural.
- **2026-05-18 — Tier 2: empty-state filter recipes on /browse (commit f3614c0).** New `src/app/browse/recipes.ts` pure helper `getFilterRecipes({examId, pyqYears})` returns up to 3 ordered chips. Year-dependent recipes degrade gracefully when not enough years are available; defensively sorts pyqYears descending. Recipes: 🎯 HARD only · 📅 Last 2 PYQ years · 🔥 HARD from latest year. All scoped via cookie-backed `loadActiveExamContext` (loaded only when `!filtered` so filtered renders pay no extra DB cost). `FilterRecipes` server component renders the chip row + a closing hint line ("Or open the filter panel to combine subject, chapter, year, and difficulty"). 9 helper tests covering URL composition + fallbacks. Direct fix for the "filter sidebar is the most powerful UI element on /browse and invisible to first-time users" gap from the Tier 1 analysis.
- **2026-05-18 — Tier 4 polish: Present-mode discoverability on /notes (commit b8c90a5).** The Present-mode keyboard shortcuts (←/→/Space/F/Esc) shipped in the original Present-mode build but lived only in CLAUDE.md — a teacher in front of a class couldn't discover them without prompting. NotePresenter trigger button bumped (bigger, `border-2`, `text-sm font-semibold`, label "Present" → "Present mode"); added `title` tooltip + descriptive `aria-label`; new keyboard-hint chip row below the button with real `<kbd>` glyphs (`← → · F · Esc`, hidden on mobile to save space). Both chapter index pages got refreshed "Built for the digital board" callouts with the keyboard shortcuts inline. Guide-side Present mode (adapting `NotePresenter` for `/guide` playbook detail pages) explicitly deferred to a separate ship (~2-3 hour build).
- **2026-05-18 — Click-to-reveal answer on QuestionCard (commit 573d04b).** **Superseded by the 2026-05-25 entry — admin bypass removed.** The green "Correct" badge + green left-border on the correct option used to render the moment a card expanded, so anonymous students couldn't self-test against PYQs — the answer was given before they could think. Now: non-admin viewers see 4 plain option buttons + a centered "Tap an option to check your answer" hint; clicking any option reveals correctness on ALL options + highlights the user's pick (green if correct, red + "Your pick" if wrong). Re-clickable — switching picks moves the red highlight, green stays on the right answer. Picked state resets when the card collapses (encourages fresh re-attempts). `isAdmin` short-circuits the reveal — admins see the answer by default (preserves the scan-content workflow; the per-question edit page is the right surface for content verification). Options render as `<button>` for non-admins, `<div>` for admins to avoid invalid nested-button HTML (the ZoomableImage close button is the inner button). Solution link unchanged — independent escape hatch for "I want the answer without committing to a pick".
- **2026-05-18 — Question reports feature (migration 0024 + reporter dialog + admin triage, commit 80b603a).** First in-app reporting loop. Migration `0024_question_reports.sql` adds the `question_reports` table with 2 enums (`report_category` 8 values · `report_status` 5 values), indexes including partial unique `(reported_by, question_id) WHERE status='open'` blocking duplicate open reports, and 4 RLS policies (insert authenticated-as-self · select own-reports · select admin-own-org · update admin-own-org). `org_id` is denormalized from `question.org_id` at INSERT (helper-set, not trigger) so the admin triage query is one indexed lookup, no JOIN. New `src/lib/reports/` helpers: `types.ts` (enums, labels, validators, length caps), `createReport.ts` (RLS-bound question lookup → org-id denorm → INSERT → 23505→`duplicate_open_report`, discriminated union return), `listReports.ts` (admin triage list with PostgREST nested-relation flattening), `updateReport.ts` (admin status transitions, auto-stamps `resolved_at` + `resolved_by` on terminal statuses, clears on revert). API: `POST /api/questions/[id]/reports` (signed-in only → 401 for anon) and `PATCH /api/reports/[id]` (admin only via `requireAdmin`). Reporter UI: new `ReportQuestionDialog` Radix island at the bottom of the QuestionCard expanded view — logged-in form (category dropdown + details textarea + char counter) → toast → "Reported" lock; anon gets sign-in prompt + mailto fallback (preserves existing Footer mailto escape). Admin triage: `/dashboard/reports` Server Component with status filter chips + severity-coloured category chips + in-place status dropdown + resolution-note field; `/dashboard` Quick Actions gain a "Question reports" card with live red open-count badge (head count, no JOIN). 13 new tests (6 createReport + 7 updateReport including RLS cross-org denial + duplicate blocking + post-resolve re-file + status stamps). Anti-spam: logged-in only + one open report per (user, question) — partial unique index does the work, no rate-limit infra wired yet.
- **2026-05-19 — `/guide/nda-history` 5-section strategy guide shipped end-to-end + Template B *variant 4* (tier-style strands) registered + `/guide/nda` bumped 6→7 cards.** Seventh guide subject under NDA. **10 indexable pages**: history landing + 5 sections (strategy, playbooks index, **timeline-and-pairs (NEW page kind)**, trends, traps) + **4 chapter playbook detail pages** at /playbooks/[slug] pre-rendered via generateStaticParams. **Template B variant 4** chosen after analysis exposed the first within-Template-B subject where execution-mode strands fail. Bank shape forced FOUR History-specific divergences. **(1) Tier-style strands (NEW within Template B)** = Cornerstone (Modern India alone = 122 q · 47% · 34% HARD — uniquely dominant chapter; no other NDA subject has one chapter at this bank share) / Foundation Recall (Ancient + Medieval = 97 q · 37% · 28% HARD avg — 75% / 64% pure recall, dates rare at 5–6%) / Quick-Win (World History = 41 q · 16% · 20% HARD — date-anchored 39%, lowest %HARD). Bio/Geo-style Recall/Apply/Verify forced lopsided cuts because History's execution modes are UNIFORM across all 4 chapters (multi-statement 70 q × 4 ch · 43% HARD; date-anchored 61 q × 4 ch · 43% HARD — don't partition chapters). So strands group by STRATEGIC PRIORITY, not execution mode. Recall is still universally stable; what bent is "second slot bends to execution mode" → "second slot bends to tier" when execution mode is bank-wide-uniform. **(2) %HARD non-flat but Modern's HARD pool is the chapter's primary content** — top-2 sub HARD concentration: Modern 63% (Freedom Movement 19 + 19thC Reform 7), Medieval 40% (DIFFUSE 3-3-3-3-2-1), Ancient 67%, World 63%. Modern's "concentrated HARD" pool IS Freedom Movement (56 q · 46% of chapter), which cannot legitimately be skipped → Template C's DrillPosture "skip HARD subs" lever fails on the dominant chapter. **(3) `/timeline-and-pairs` = History-specific subject artefact** — analogue of /vocab-families /formulas /common-compounds /reference-tables. **Multi-domain** like Bio/Geo: 5 themed clusters · ~95 entries (Era timeline ~42 absolute dates BCE → 1947+ for date-anchored q; Rulers ↔ dynasty 21 entries for Ancient+Medieval recall; Reformers ↔ movement ↔ text 17 entries for 19thC Reform 17 q · 41% HARD; Scholars ↔ texts 20 entries for Ancient Literature 12 q · 42% HARD + Medieval literature; Viceroys + British Acts ↔ year 18 entries for British Admin 16 q · 38% HARD). Rendered via new `HistoryReferenceTables` component (7th multi-domain parallel). **(4) Traps page bucketed by GENRE not tier** — paired-fact swap (reformer↔movement, ruler↔dynasty, scholar↔text, Harappan site↔feature, Act↔year, Mahamatta function) / chronology-sequence (traveller-by-era, Gandhian satyagraha order, EIC dates, IR invention chronology) / multi-statement Verify (partial-credit, universal-claim, match-list misalignment, WWI impact). Bucketing by genre because the FIX (memorise pair table / anchor on absolute dates / judge each statement independently) maps to the genre regardless of chapter — different from Geography which bucketed by strategy strand (R/A/V). **Template A definitively ruled out**: largest cross-chapter topical lever is Reformers/Movements 27 q × 2 ch (fails A's 40 q × ≥4 ch gate). Dynasties/Rulers 20 × 4 ch, Books/Authors 15 × 4 ch — all too thin. **Template C ruled out** for two stacked reasons: top-2 HARD concentration 63–67% looks like C's pattern, but the dominant chapter's HARD pool is the chapter's primary content; AND Medieval is genuinely DIFFUSE (3-3-3-3-2-1). DrillPosture has weak leverage. **Playbook granularity 1:1 with chapters** (4 playbooks — smallest of any NDA guide, because History only has 4 chapters). 8 worked-example UUIDs SQL-picked against the live 260-q bank (2 per chapter — most-recent HARD where exists). **Trends headline = "paper has NOT consistently hardened"** (same framing as Chem/Bio/Geo) — %HARD bounces 19%→42%→10%, 2021 peak, 2026-1 lowest, recent avg ~26%. PLUS distinctive secondary callout: **chapter mix shifted dramatically** — Modern dominated 2017–20 (~55% of paper), Ancient surged 9–10 q/yr from 2022 onwards from a ~2 q/yr baseline. The "Modern is 47% of bank" stat is skewed by old papers; recent papers are closer to 30–40% Modern. **Generic infra**: no GuideShell/SideNav changes needed; DriftTable's `rowLabel="Chapter"` reused. **New components**: `HistoryReferenceTables` (7th multi-domain parallel) + `RelatedHistoryPlaybooks` (7th parallel — link path hard-coded per guide). **Plumbing**: sitemap +/guide/nda-history + 5 sections + 4 detail pages (HISTORY_PLAYBOOK_SLUGS); Footer adds NDA History Guide link (Landmark icon); /browse Hero adds nda-history pill; /guide/nda-maths landing adds 6th cross-link banner (Landmark); /guide/nda landing bumped 6→7 cards (sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 xl:grid-cols-3 unchanged — 7 fits naturally in 2×3+1 or 3+3+1); /nda exam-home GUIDE_PREVIEWS bumped 6→7; questionResources.ts extended to map NDA History chapter → /guide/nda-history/playbooks/{slug} so /browse cards get a "Playbook: Modern India" chip. **TDD-first**: wrote `tests/guide-nda-history-playbooks.test.ts` (mirror of english + physics + chemistry + biology + geography tests) BEFORE the data modules — 5 invariants including live-taxonomy resolution. All 701/701 tests pass; prepush clean (149 static pages). **The variant lesson (updated)**: Template B's strand axis bent for the first time. English/Chemistry/Biology/Geography all split chapters by execution mode (R/Rule/Reason · R/Rule/Calculate · R/Apply/Verify · R/Apply/Verify). History's execution modes are bank-wide-uniform (multi-statement 70 q × 4 ch and date-anchored 61 q × 4 ch don't partition chapters) — so the strand axis bent to strategic priority (Cornerstone / Foundation Recall / Quick-Win). The "Recall is universally stable" lesson holds in spirit: there's still a recall-anchored strand (Foundation Recall), but the SECOND and THIRD slots bent to tier rather than execution mode. When the bank has no clean execution-mode chapter seam, tier-style by strategic priority is the fallback.
- **2026-05-19 (later) — `/guide/nda-polity` 5-section strategy guide shipped end-to-end + Template B *variant 5* (tier-style strands, INVERTED Wildcard) registered + `/guide/nda` bumped 7→8 cards (commit 21b6b2f).** **Eighth** guide subject under NDA — completes guides for every NDA-eligible subject except Economics + Current Affairs. **12 indexable pages**: polity landing + 5 sections (strategy, playbooks index, reference-tables, trends, traps) + **4 chapter playbook detail pages** at /playbooks/[slug] pre-rendered via generateStaticParams. **Template B variant 5** chosen — second use of tier-style strands within Template B (after History 5 days earlier), BUT with the **third tier INVERTED**: where History's Quick-Win is the smallest AND easiest chapter (World History 41 q · 20% HARD), Polity's third tier is the smallest BUT HARDEST chapter (World Polity 12 q · 42% HARD · 50% multi-statement) — relabelled **"Specialist Wildcard"** to capture the small-and-high-stakes profile (opposite framing from Quick-Win's small-and-easy). Strand split: Cornerstone (Government Structure 36 q · 40% · 17% HARD — carries 6 of 17 bank HARDs) / Foundation Recall (Indian Constitution + FR/DPSP/Local Governance = 42 q · 47% · 14% HARD avg) / Specialist Wildcard (World Polity 12 q · 13% · 42% HARD). **Tier-style picked over execution-mode** for two reasons: (a) bank is tiny (90 q · 4 ch · avg 5 q/paper — smallest GAT section we've guided), so strategic-priority labels are more actionable than skill-bucket labels at this scale; (b) multi-statement partition is partial (4.5% / 15% / 30.6% / 50% across chapters — Govt Structure has both heavy multi-stmt AND single-fact recall, doesn't cleanly fit one execution-mode bucket). Template A ruled out (max cross-chapter lever article-citation 28 q × 3 ch fails 40-q gate); Template C ruled out (only 1 chapter clearly >15% HARD AND the chapter is too small for DrillPosture to add signal). **Polity-specific subject artefact = `/reference-tables`** — multi-domain like Bio/Geo/History: 4 themed clusters · 80 entries (Key Articles ↔ Subject 32 anchors — bank's #1 cross-chapter lever; Constitutional Amendments ↔ Year ↔ Theme 18; Constitutional Bodies ↔ Function ↔ Article 19; Parts ↔ Schedules ↔ Content 11). Rendered via new `PolityReferenceTables` component (8th multi-domain parallel after Bio + Geo + History). **STRATEGY_HEADLINE SQL-verified** per [[per-paper-q-counts]] recipe: paperQ 5 · 20 max marks · target 14 marks · 80% accuracy on 5 attempts. **Trap-page NEW genre: PROCEDURAL CONFUSION** — Polity-UNIQUE bucket alongside paired-fact swap + multi-statement Verify. Money Bill vs Finance Bill (Article 110 vs 117), Speaker vs President powers (Speaker adjourns sine die; President summons/prorogues/dissolves), original vs appellate jurisdiction (Article 131 vs 132–134), constitutional vs statutory body (Lokpal + NHRC + CIC are statutory NOT constitutional — common HARD distractor). 11 trap shapes total. **Worked-example UUIDs** SQL-picked (2 per chapter, HARD-where-exists): 2026-1 Speaker sine-die + HC territorial jurisdictions; 2026-1 Parts pairs + 2018 provisions effective 26 Nov 1949; 2022 Article 243G + 2019 Fifth Schedule; 2026-1 UN peacekeeping + 2025 universal franchise chronology. **Generic infra**: no GuideShell/SideNav changes needed; DriftTable's `rowLabel="Chapter"` reused. **New components**: `PolityReferenceTables` (8th multi-domain parallel) + `RelatedPolityPlaybooks` (8th RelatedX parallel — link path hard-coded per guide). **Plumbing**: sitemap +/guide/nda-polity + 5 sections + 4 detail pages (POLITY_PLAYBOOK_SLUGS); Footer adds NDA Polity Guide link (Scale icon); /browse Hero adds nda-polity pill; /guide/nda-maths landing adds 7th cross-link banner (Scale); /guide/nda landing bumped 7→8 cards (xl:grid-cols-3 → xl:grid-cols-4) + "All eight guides" copy update; /nda exam-home GUIDE_PREVIEWS bumped 7→8; questionResources.ts extended to map NDA Polity chapter → /guide/nda-polity/playbooks/{slug} so /browse cards get a "Playbook: …" chip. **TDD-first**: wrote `tests/guide-nda-polity-playbooks.test.ts` (mirror of english + physics + chemistry + biology + geography + history tests) BEFORE the data modules — 5 invariants including live-taxonomy resolution. tests/question-resources.test.ts updated (Polity removed from no-guide list + per-chapter playbook tests added). All 707/707 tests pass; prepush clean. **The variant lesson (updated)**: Tier-style strands can have INVERTED third-tier semantics. History's Quick-Win was small+easy; Polity's Specialist Wildcard is small+HARDEST — same Cornerstone + Foundation Recall pattern, opposite third-tier profile. Don't reflexively reuse "Quick-Win" label when adopting tier-style; verify the smallest chapter's %HARD against the rest of the subject first. Also: tier-style isn't only for History-shaped subjects (modes-uniform AND one dominant chapter at 47% of bank) — it's also the right pick when the bank is small enough that strategic-priority labels are more actionable than execution-mode labels even when modes partly partition.
- **2026-05-19 (post-Polity) — NDA Economics + NDA Current Affairs guides explicitly deferred (no ship planned).** Both subjects ruled out for standalone `/guide/<exam>/<subject>` treatment after the Polity ship completed the 8th NDA guide. **Economics (24 q · 1 chapter · 3 subtopics)** is below any sensible bank-size threshold for a guide — would produce 1 playbook card + a reference table with maybe 5 entries, no meaningful strand split, no editorial signal beyond what `/browse` already shows. **Current Affairs (180 q · 8 chapters · 31 subtopics)** is bank-shape-large-enough (similar to Geography's 345 q · 7 ch or History's 260 q · 4 ch), and a tier-style strand split would be plausible (Defence & Military 19 q · International Affairs 18 q · Govt Schemes 18 q · S&T 15 q · Sports 13 q · etc.). What kills it is **content half-life**: Current Affairs questions reference SPECIFIC PYQ-year events (2024 election results, 2023 G20 hosting, 2022 ISRO Chairman appointment, named operation codenames, named-yatra leaders) that DON'T recur as stable levers the way Article 14 or AM-GM do. The right shape for Current Affairs prep is a continuously-updated revision document (year-by-year named-event reference), not a static strategy guide — and that's a different product. If/when a continuously-curated CA revision artefact ships (likely tied to a yearly editorial cycle, not the strategy-guide pattern), revisit this decision. **What students get instead**: `/browse` already supports chapter + subtopic + PYQ-year filtering for both subjects (drill 2024 Current Affairs Defence — works today); the `/nda` exam-home Hero links into the bank for these subjects, just without a Guide card. ROADMAP's "NDA other-subject guides" section pruned to remove the 7-remaining note and the misframe that thin subjects "may not justify a full guide" → explicit defer with rationale. (Same-day override below for Economics; CA defer stands.)
- **2026-05-19 (later) — NDA Economics override: shipped as a single-page landing (`/guide/nda-economics`).** Reverses the same-day defer for Economics only (CA defer stands). User asked to analyse, and on review opted for a thin one-page artefact over no-ship. **Why the override is honest**: a multi-route Template B/C guide would still be parody at 24 q · 1 ch · 3 sub · 1.5 q/paper · ~6 marks max — every template-fit analysis above holds. BUT the **bank's named-fact-recall lever IS the Five Year Plans timeline** (18 of 24 PUBLIC questions live in that one subtopic; 75% of bank by mass), and shipping that reference + a chapter/subtopic breakdown + the strategic cap on a single page lets students prep proportionally without us pretending the subject deserves more shelf space than it has. **Structure**: ONE page only. No /strategy, no /playbooks, no /trends, no /traps, no /principles, no /reference-tables, no per-playbook detail pages, no STRATEGY_HEADLINE (the numbers would read as parody — `paperQ 1.5 × 4 = 6 marks, target 4, attempt 1–2` is not strategy). NO DB tags table (cross-chapter axis is impossible at 1 chapter). NO new components — Plan timeline renders as an inline `<table>` (simpler than CommonCompoundsTable; single-domain, ~15 rows including 3 inter-Plan gaps). **Content**: hero with 4 stats (24 q · ~1.5 q/paper · ~6 max marks · 41.7% HARD), an honest-stance callout that names the cap explicitly ("~6 marks of 600-mark GAT, about 1%"), a 3-row subtopic table, the 12-Plan + 3-Plan-gap timeline as the recall anchor, a 4-step exam-day playbook ("attempt the easy 1, be cautious on the second, skip current-affairs spillover, target 3–4 of 6"), two BrowseLink CTAs (all 24 + HARD-only), and a "why this guide is one page" closing section that names the asymmetry. **Plumbing**: sitemap +1 entry at priority 0.7 (vs 0.8 for full landings — flags the thinness); Footer +1 link with TrendingUp icon; /browse Hero +1 pill; /guide/nda card grid bumped 8→9 (stays `xl:grid-cols-4` for a 4+4+1 layout — copy updated "Eight evidence-led" → "Nine evidence-led"); /nda exam-home GUIDE_PREVIEWS +1 entry (24 q · 9-grid); /guide/nda-maths cross-link banner +1 row; `questionResources.ts` extended to map NDA Economics chapter → /guide/nda-economics (Template A pattern — single landing, not per-chapter playbook). **TDD-first**: extended `tests/question-resources.test.ts` BEFORE the code change (2 new tests + removed Economics from the no-guide list; failed red, then green). 32/32 in that suite pass. Notes-lint clean (no /notes content changed). **The honest framing earned its place**: every other NDA guide is 10–22 indexable pages. NDA Economics is 1. Same template tree, intentionally thin terminal node — different from a stale defer.
- **2026-05-19 (later, post-Economics) — NDA Current Affairs override + Template D registered: shipped as `/guide/nda-current-affairs` single-page theme-prep checklist.** Reverses the same-day defer for Current Affairs after empirical re-analysis. User pushed back on the initial defer with a sharper challenge: "is there a theme of topics on which students can prepare?" — a productive reframe that turned the analysis from "memorise these facts? no" to "are there stable SHAPES? yes". **Half-life evidence** (probed against the live bank): of 67 explicit-year mentions in CA questions, **90% land within 12 months of the paper** (gap = -1: 36% · gap = 0: 54% · gap = -2: 6% · outliers: 4%). Of 13 Military Exercise PYQs across 5 paper-years, **13 different exercises** are named — almost zero recurrence (Garuda and Malabar each appear in exactly one paper). Of ~14 well-known flagship schemes checked against 33 Govt Schemes PYQs, only **3 match — each in one paper** (and the MGNREGA hit is a year-stamped count question "districts covered as on 2024-25"). Empirical confirmation: the user was right — the bank's content goes stale. **BUT the user's reframe found the second half of the answer**: while specific facts rotate, **subtopic THEMES are stable** — 8 subtopics appear in 5+ years of papers (Cricket 8 yrs · Governance/UT 7 yrs · Health/Edu schemes 6 yrs · India bilateral 5 yrs · Intl Orgs 6 yrs · Military Exercises 5 yrs · Space Tech 5 yrs · Other Sports 5 yrs), carrying **86 q (48% of bank)**. The PREP HANDLE is THESE RECURRING SHAPES, not the historical facts. **NEW: Template D — Theme-Prep Checklist for short-half-life subjects.** Strand axis is **recurrence strength** (not skill, not strategic priority): Anchor themes (5+ year recurrence · 86 q · ~5 q/paper) → Recurring themes (3–4 year recurrence · 74 q · ~3 q/paper) → Occasional themes (1–2 year recurrence · 20 q · drill if time permits). **Anchor theme cards** carry: (a) question SHAPE — one sentence describing the recurring stem pattern (durable, sourced from real bank stems); (b) prep CHECKLIST — 3–6 fact-CATEGORIES to harvest from this year's news (durable directives, NOT specific facts); (c) optional durable-anchors callout (G7 nations, UN agency HQs, ICC Hall of Fame Indians — the 15–20% structural-fact subset that DOES stay stable); (d) drill BrowseLink for SHAPE practice. **Structure**: ONE page only. No /strategy, no /playbooks, no /trends, no /traps, no /reference-tables, no per-playbook detail pages. Single page contains: hero with 4 stats (180 q · ~10 q/paper · ~40 max marks · 12.2% HARD) + half-life directive callout (load-bearing — "Drill this bank for SHAPE, not for FACTS" with what-bank-gives / what-you-bring two-card layout) + 8-row CHAPTER_TABLE + 8 anchor-theme `<article>` cards + 16-row recurring themes table + 7-entry occasional themes chip grid + test-day plan callout (target 24 of ~40 marks, attempt 7 skip 3, watch the multi-statement trap) + 3 BrowseLink CTAs (all 180 + last 3 years + HARD only) + "why this guide is one page" closing rationale. NO DB tags table (cross-chapter axis impossible — each chapter is a different subject domain: Defence ≠ Sports ≠ Awards). NO new components — anchor theme cards render as inline JSX. **Plumbing**: sitemap +1 entry at priority **0.6** (vs Economics's 0.7 — thinner-flag because the page is shape-calibration not memorise-this); Footer +1 link with Newspaper icon; /browse Hero +1 pill; /guide/nda card grid bumped 9→10 (stays xl:grid-cols-4 for a 4+4+2 layout; copy updated "Nine evidence-led" → "Ten evidence-led"); /nda exam-home GUIDE_PREVIEWS +1 entry (180 q · 10-grid; "All nine" → "All ten"); /guide/nda-maths cross-link banner +1 row (Newspaper); `questionResources.ts` extended — Current Affairs removed from the default-null fallback, all 8 CA chapters now map to `/guide/nda-current-affairs` (single-page pattern, label "NDA Current Affairs strategy"). **TDD-first**: wrote `tests/guide-nda-current-affairs-themes.test.ts` BEFORE the data module — 10 invariants including slug uniqueness across all 3 buckets + qCount sums to 180 + recurrence-window definitions + live taxonomy resolution. tests/question-resources.test.ts updated (CA removed from no-guide list + 8 chapter→landing tests + null-subtopic fallback test added). All **727/727 tests pass; prepush clean (161 static pages)**. **The Template D lesson**: when the empirical evidence undermines a fact-memorise guide proposal, don't reach for "ship a thin landing with structural facts only" (the durability-tier proposal I'd drafted first — also wrong, because military exercises and schemes ARE largely year-specific). Instead, find the second axis (recurring SHAPES) and build the guide around prep DIRECTIVES, not prep FACTS. The bank is reframed as a shape-calibrator with explicit half-life framing in the copy. Earned its place as Template D because no prior template handles short-half-life content; the strand axis is genuinely new (recurrence-strength); and the framing rule "drill for shape, harvest facts externally" is non-transferable to subjects where facts ARE durable (Economics's Five Year Plans, Bio's diseases↔pathogens). User-driven turn from defer → ship via the "is there a theme" reframe is the load-bearing process learning here — captures the value of pushing back on a defer with a precise counter-question rather than a vague "are you sure?".

Avg 30.6 q/chapter and 12.2 q/sub. **Subject cleanup status**: MHT-CET Maths is the 12th delta-cleanup pass; combined bank cleanup grand total now **132 ch · 5,655 q · 449 sub**. Only MHT-CET Chemistry (775 q) and MHT-CET Physics (792 q) remain pending — both have grown 3× since their pre-2024 baseline and will need a fresh fragmentation probe + Phase B/C/D pass (extraction-prompt canonical-list discipline NOT yet verified for these subjects since they were never originally cleaned). Flagged dedup escapes (out of cleanup scope): in the orphan chapter, two pairs had near-identical stems with different question_ids (32f969fb/6b8d47fb both `sin(cot⁻¹(x)) = cos(tan⁻¹(1+x))`; 4365d1ca/bc9eb718 both `tan⁻¹((1-x)/(1+x)) = (1/2)tan⁻¹x`) — likely content_hash bypass via whitespace variation; separate from taxonomy work.
